namespace {

/**
 * @brief Obtiene el consumo pico de memoria fisica del proceso (Peak Working Set / RSS) en bytes.
 */
std::size_t get_os_peak_rss_bytes() {
#ifdef _WIN32
    PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS pmc;
    if (GetProcessMemoryInfo(GetCurrentProcess(), &pmc, sizeof(pmc))) {
        return static_cast<std::size_t>(pmc.PeakWorkingSetSize);
    }
#endif
    return 0;
}

/**
 * @brief Simulador de jerarquia de cache L1D (32 KB, 8-way, 64B line) y L2 (256 KB, 8-way, 64B line)
 *        con politica de reemplazo LRU y clasificacion de accesos secuenciales vs. indirectos.
 */
class CacheHierarchySimulator {
public:
    static constexpr std::size_t LINE_BYTES = 64; // 16 ints de 4 bytes por linea de cache
    static constexpr std::size_t L1_SETS = 64;    // 64 sets * 8 ways * 64B = 32 KB L1D
    static constexpr std::size_t L1_WAYS = 8;
    static constexpr std::size_t L2_SETS = 512;   // 512 sets * 8 ways * 64B = 256 KB L2
    static constexpr std::size_t L2_WAYS = 8;

    std::uint64_t total_accesses = 0;
    std::uint64_t sequential_accesses = 0;
    std::uint64_t indirect_accesses = 0;
    std::uint64_t l1_hits = 0;
    std::uint64_t l1_misses = 0;
    std::uint64_t l2_hits = 0;
    std::uint64_t l2_misses = 0;

    CacheHierarchySimulator() {
        reset();
    }

    void reset() {
        total_accesses = 0;
        sequential_accesses = 0;
        indirect_accesses = 0;
        l1_hits = 0;
        l1_misses = 0;
        l2_hits = 0;
        l2_misses = 0;
        tick_ = 0;
        for (auto& set : l1_tags_) set.fill(UINT64_MAX);
        for (auto& set : l1_lru_)  set.fill(0);
        for (auto& set : l2_tags_) set.fill(UINT64_MAX);
        for (auto& set : l2_lru_)  set.fill(0);
    }

    inline void access(const void* addr, bool is_sequential) {
        total_accesses++;
        if (is_sequential) {
            sequential_accesses++;
        } else {
            indirect_accesses++;
        }

        const std::uint64_t line_addr = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(addr) / LINE_BYTES;
        tick_++;

        // 1. Consulta en L1D
        const std::size_t l1_set = static_cast<std::size_t>(line_addr % L1_SETS);
        for (std::size_t w = 0; w < L1_WAYS; ++w) {
            if (l1_tags_[l1_set][w] == line_addr) {
                l1_hits++;
                l1_lru_[l1_set][w] = tick_;
                return;
            }
        }

        // Fallo en L1D -> insertar en L1D (reemplazo LRU) y consultar L2
        l1_misses++;
        std::size_t l1_victim = 0;
        for (std::size_t w = 1; w < L1_WAYS; ++w) {
            if (l1_lru_[l1_set][w] < l1_lru_[l1_set][l1_victim]) {
                l1_victim = w;
            }
        }
        l1_tags_[l1_set][l1_victim] = line_addr;
        l1_lru_[l1_set][l1_victim] = tick_;

        // 2. Consulta en L2
        const std::size_t l2_set = static_cast<std::size_t>(line_addr % L2_SETS);
        for (std::size_t w = 0; w < L2_WAYS; ++w) {
            if (l2_tags_[l2_set][w] == line_addr) {
                l2_hits++;
                l2_lru_[l2_set][w] = tick_;
                return;
            }
        }

        // Fallo en L2 -> insertar en L2 (reemplazo LRU)
        l2_misses++;
        std::size_t l2_victim = 0;
        for (std::size_t w = 1; w < L2_WAYS; ++w) {
            if (l2_lru_[l2_set][w] < l2_lru_[l2_set][l2_victim]) {
                l2_victim = w;
            }
        }
        l2_tags_[l2_set][l2_victim] = line_addr;
        l2_lru_[l2_set][l2_victim] = tick_;
    }

private:
    std::uint64_t tick_ = 0;
    std::array<std::array<std::uint64_t, L1_WAYS>, L1_SETS> l1_tags_{};
    std::array<std::array<std::uint64_t, L1_WAYS>, L1_SETS> l1_lru_{};
    std::array<std::array<std::uint64_t, L2_WAYS>, L2_SETS> l2_tags_{};
    std::array<std::array<std::uint64_t, L2_WAYS>, L2_SETS> l2_lru_{};
};

// Instrumentacion de DC3 sobre el simulador de cache
void sim_dc3_radixPass(
    const int* a, int* b, const int* r, int n, int K, CacheHierarchySimulator& sim
) {
    std::vector<int> c(K + 1, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i <= K; ++i) {
        sim.access(&c[i], true);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&a[i], true);          // Lectura secuencial de a[i]
        int idx = a[i];
        sim.access(&r[idx], false);       // Salto indirecto a r[a[i]]
        int key = r[idx];
        sim.access(&c[key], false);       // Incremento indirecto en cubeta c[key]
        c[key]++;
    }
    for (int i = 0, sum = 0; i <= K; ++i) {
        sim.access(&c[i], true);
        int t = c[i];
        c[i] = sum;
        sum += t;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&a[i], true);          // Lectura secuencial de a[i]
        int idx = a[i];
        sim.access(&r[idx], false);       // Salto indirecto a r[a[i]]
        int key = r[idx];
        sim.access(&c[key], false);       // Consulta/post-incremento de puntero de cubeta
        int dst = c[key]++;
        sim.access(&b[dst], false);       // Escritura dispersa en la cubeta destino de b[]
        b[dst] = idx;
    }
}

void sim_dc3_suffixArray(const int* s, int* SA, int n, int K, CacheHierarchySimulator& sim) {
    if (n <= 0) return;
    if (n == 1) {
        sim.access(&SA[0], true);
        SA[0] = 0;
        return;
    }
    int n0 = (n + 2) / 3;
    int n1 = (n + 1) / 3;
    int n2 = n / 3;
    int n02 = n0 + n2;

    std::vector<int> s12(n02 + 3, 0);
    std::vector<int> SA12(n02 + 3, 0);

    for (int i = 0, j = 0; i < n + (n0 - n1); ++i) {
        if (i % 3 != 0) {
            sim.access(&s12[j], true);
            s12[j++] = i;
        }
    }

    sim_dc3_radixPass(s12.data(), SA12.data(), s + 2, n02, K, sim);
    sim_dc3_radixPass(SA12.data(), s12.data(), s + 1, n02, K, sim);
    sim_dc3_radixPass(s12.data(), SA12.data(), s,     n02, K, sim);

    int name = 0, c0 = -1, c1 = -1, c2 = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < n02; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA12[i], true);
        int pos = SA12[i];
        sim.access(&s[pos], false);
        sim.access(&s[pos + 1], true);
        sim.access(&s[pos + 2], true);
        if (s[pos] != c0 || s[pos + 1] != c1 || s[pos + 2] != c2) {
            name++;
            c0 = s[pos];
            c1 = s[pos + 1];
            c2 = s[pos + 2];
        }
        int target = (pos % 3 == 1) ? (pos / 3) : (pos / 3 + n0);
        sim.access(&s12[target], false);
        s12[target] = name;
    }

    if (name < n02) {
        sim_dc3_suffixArray(s12.data(), SA12.data(), n02, name, sim);
        for (int i = 0; i < n02; ++i) {
            sim.access(&SA12[i], true);
            sim.access(&s12[SA12[i]], false);
            s12[SA12[i]] = i + 1;
        }
    } else {
        for (int i = 0; i < n02; ++i) {
            sim.access(&s12[i], true);
            sim.access(&SA12[s12[i] - 1], false);
            SA12[s12[i] - 1] = i;
        }
    }

    std::vector<int> s0(n0, 0);
    std::vector<int> SA0(n0, 0);
    for (int i = 0, j = 0; i < n02; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA12[i], true);
        if (SA12[i] < n0) {
            sim.access(&s0[j], true);
            s0[j++] = 3 * SA12[i];
        }
    }
    sim_dc3_radixPass(s0.data(), SA0.data(), s, n0, K, sim);

    for (int p = 0, t = n0 - n1, k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
        sim.access(&SA12[t], true);
        sim.access(&SA0[p], true);
        int i = (SA12[t] < n0) ? (SA12[t] * 3 + 1) : ((SA12[t] - n0) * 3 + 2);
        int j = SA0[p];

        sim.access(&s[i], false);
        sim.access(&s[j], false);
        bool sa12_smaller = false;
        if (SA12[t] < n0) {
            sim.access(&s12[SA12[t] + n0], false);
            sim.access(&s12[j / 3], false);
            sa12_smaller = eda::leq(s[i], s12[SA12[t] + n0], s[j], s12[j / 3]);
        } else {
            sim.access(&s[i + 1], true);
            sim.access(&s[j + 1], true);
            sim.access(&s12[SA12[t] - n0 + 1], false);
            sim.access(&s12[j / 3 + n0], false);
            sa12_smaller = eda::leq(s[i], s[i + 1], s12[SA12[t] - n0 + 1], s[j], s[j + 1], s12[j / 3 + n0]);
        }

        sim.access(&SA[k], true);
        if (sa12_smaller) {
            SA[k] = i;
            t++;
            if (t == n02) {
                for (k++; p < n0; p++, k++) {
                    sim.access(&SA0[p], true);
                    sim.access(&SA[k], true);
                    SA[k] = SA0[p];
                }
            }
        } else {
            SA[k] = j;
            p++;
            if (p == n0) {
                for (k++; t < n02; t++, k++) {
                    sim.access(&SA12[t], true);
                    sim.access(&SA[k], true);
                    SA[k] = (SA12[t] < n0) ? (SA12[t] * 3 + 1) : ((SA12[t] - n0) * 3 + 2);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Instrumentacion de SA-IS sobre el simulador de cache
void sim_sais_getBuckets(const int* s, int* bkt, int n, int K, bool end, CacheHierarchySimulator& sim) {
    for (int i = 0; i <= K; ++i) {
        sim.access(&bkt[i], true);
        bkt[i] = 0;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&s[i], true);
        sim.access(&bkt[s[i]], false);
        bkt[s[i]]++;
    }
    int sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i <= K; ++i) {
        sim.access(&bkt[i], true);
        sum += bkt[i];
        bkt[i] = end ? (sum - 1) : (sum - bkt[i]);
    }
}

void sim_sais_induceSAl(const int* t, int* SA, const int* s, int* bkt, int n, int K, CacheHierarchySimulator& sim) {
    sim_sais_getBuckets(s, bkt, n, K, false, sim);
    for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        int j = SA[i] - 1;
        if (j >= 0) {
            sim.access(&t[j], false);
            if (t[j] == eda::L_TYPE) {
                sim.access(&s[j], false);
                int c = s[j];
                sim.access(&bkt[c], false);
                int dst = bkt[c]++;
                sim.access(&SA[dst], true); // Avance contiguo dentro de la subcubeta L
                SA[dst] = j;
            }
        }
    }
}

void sim_sais_induceSAs(const int* t, int* SA, const int* s, int* bkt, int n, int K, CacheHierarchySimulator& sim) {
    sim_sais_getBuckets(s, bkt, n, K, true, sim);
    for (int i = n; i >= 0; --i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        int j = SA[i] - 1;
        if (j >= 0) {
            sim.access(&t[j], false);
            if (t[j] == eda::S_TYPE) {
                sim.access(&s[j], false);
                int c = s[j];
                sim.access(&bkt[c], false);
                int dst = bkt[c]--;
                sim.access(&SA[dst], true); // Retroceso contiguo dentro de la subcubeta S
                SA[dst] = j;
            }
        }
    }
}

void sim_sais_core(const int* s, int* SA, int n, int K, CacheHierarchySimulator& sim) {
    std::vector<int> t(n + 1, 0);
    sim.access(&t[n], true);
    t[n] = eda::S_TYPE;
    for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        sim.access(&s[i], true);
        sim.access(&s[i + 1], true);
        sim.access(&t[i], true);
        t[i] = (s[i] < s[i + 1] || (s[i] == s[i + 1] && t[i + 1] == eda::S_TYPE)) ? eda::S_TYPE : eda::L_TYPE;
    }

    std::vector<int> bkt(K + 1, 0);
    sim_sais_getBuckets(s, bkt.data(), n, K, true, sim);
    for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        SA[i] = -1;
    }
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&t[i], true);
        if (eda::isLMS(i, t.data())) {
            sim.access(&s[i], true);
            int dst = bkt[s[i]]--;
            sim.access(&SA[dst], true);
            SA[dst] = i;
        }
    }
    sim_sais_induceSAl(t.data(), SA, s, bkt.data(), n, K, sim);
    sim_sais_induceSAs(t.data(), SA, s, bkt.data(), n, K, sim);

    int n1 = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        if (eda::isLMS(SA[i], t.data())) {
            sim.access(&SA[n1], true);
            SA[n1++] = SA[i];
        }
    }

    for (int i = n1; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        SA[i] = -1;
    }
    int name = 0, prev = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < n1; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        int pos = SA[i];
        bool equal = false;
        if (prev >= 0) {
            for (int d = 0; d <= n; ++d) {
                sim.access(&s[pos + d], d > 0);
                sim.access(&s[prev + d], d > 0);
                sim.access(&t[pos + d], d > 0);
                sim.access(&t[prev + d], d > 0);
                if (s[pos + d] != s[prev + d] || t[pos + d] != t[prev + d]) {
                    equal = false;
                    break;
                }
                if (d > 0 && (eda::isLMS(pos + d, t.data()) || eda::isLMS(prev + d, t.data()))) {
                    equal = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if (!equal) {
            name++;
            prev = pos;
        }
        sim.access(&SA[n1 + pos / 2], false);
        SA[n1 + pos / 2] = name - 1;
    }

    for (int i = n, j = n; i >= n1; --i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        if (SA[i] >= 0) {
            sim.access(&SA[j], true);
            SA[j--] = SA[i];
        }
    }

    int* SA1 = SA;
    int* s1 = SA + n + 1 - n1;
    if (name < n1) {
        sim_sais_core(s1, SA1, n1 - 1, name - 1, sim);
    } else {
        for (int i = 0; i < n1; ++i) {
            sim.access(&s1[i], true);
            sim.access(&SA1[s1[i]], false);
            SA1[s1[i]] = i;
        }
    }

    sim_sais_getBuckets(s, bkt.data(), n, K, true, sim);
    for (int i = 1, j = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&t[i], true);
        if (eda::isLMS(i, t.data())) {
            sim.access(&s1[j], true);
            s1[j++] = i;
        }
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < n1; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA1[i], true);
        sim.access(&s1[SA1[i]], false);
        SA1[i] = s1[SA1[i]];
    }
    for (int i = n1; i <= n; ++i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        SA[i] = -1;
    }
    for (int i = n1 - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        sim.access(&SA[i], true);
        int j = SA[i];
        SA[i] = -1;
        sim.access(&s[j], false);
        int dst = bkt[s[j]]--;
        sim.access(&SA[dst], true);
        SA[dst] = j;
    }
    sim_sais_induceSAl(t.data(), SA, s, bkt.data(), n, K, sim);
    sim_sais_induceSAs(t.data(), SA, s, bkt.data(), n, K, sim);
}

/**
 * @brief Genera una cadena aleatoria uniforme sobre un alfabeto de tamano sigma_size.
 */
std::string generate_random_text(int n, int sigma_size, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    std::mt19937 rng(seed);
    std::uniform_int_distribution<int> dist(0, sigma_size - 1);
    std::string s(n, ' ');
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        s[i] = static_cast<char>('!' + dist(rng));
    }
    return s;
}

/**
 * @brief Genera una secuencia genomica sintetica sobre el alfabeto {A, C, G, T} con repeticiones.
 */
std::string generate_dna_sequence(int n, unsigned int seed = 1337) {
    static const char bases[4] = {'A', 'C', 'G', 'T'};
    std::mt19937 rng(seed);
    std::uniform_int_distribution<int> base_dist(0, 3);
    std::string s;
    s.reserve(n);

    const std::string motif = "GATTACAGATTACATGCA";
    while (static_cast<int>(s.size()) < n) {
        if (rng() % 7 == 0 && static_cast<int>(s.size() + motif.size()) <= n) {
            s += motif;
        } else {
            s.push_back(bases[base_dist(rng)]);
        }
    }
    s.resize(n);
    return s;
}

/**
 * @brief Genera un flujo sintetico de codigo fuente C++ (orientado a analizadores lexicos LR(1)).
 */
std::string generate_source_code(int n, unsigned int seed = 2026) {
    static const std::vector<std::string> tokens = {
        "int ", "void ", "for(int i=0;i<n;++i){", "if(s[i]<s[i+1]){",
        "return ", "std::vector<int> ", "SA[bkt[s[j]]--]=j;", "radixPass(s12,SA12,s,n02,K);",
        "else{", "} ", "while(k<n)", "const char* ", "auto ", "0;\n"
    };
    std::mt19937 rng(seed);
    std::uniform_int_distribution<std::size_t> dist(0, tokens.size() - 1);
    std::string s;
    s.reserve(n + 64);
    while (static_cast<int>(s.size()) < n) {
        s += tokens[dist(rng)];
    }
    s.resize(n);
    return s;
}

/**
 * @brief Genera una cadena altamente periodica para estresar la profundidad recursiva.
 */
std::string generate_periodic_string(int n) {
    const std::string pattern = "mississippi_mmiississippii_abracadabra_";
    std::string s;
    s.reserve(n + pattern.size());
    while (static_cast<int>(s.size()) < n) {
        s += pattern;
    }
    s.resize(n);
    return s;
}

struct BenchmarkRow {
    std::string dataset;
    int n = 0;
    double naive_ms = -1.0;
    double dc3_median_ms = 0.0;
    double dc3_mean_ms = 0.0;
    double sais_median_ms = 0.0;
    double sais_mean_ms = 0.0;
    double speedup_dc3_over_sais = 0.0;
    int dc3_max_depth = 0;
    int sais_max_depth = 0;
    double dc3_reduction_ratio = 0.0;
    double sais_reduction_ratio = 0.0;
    std::size_t dc3_peak_aux_kb = 0;
    std::size_t sais_peak_aux_kb = 0;
    double dc3_bytes_per_char = 0.0;
    double sais_bytes_per_char = 0.0;
    double sais_compact_bpc = 0.0;
};

double compute_median(std::vector<double> v) {
    if (v.empty()) return 0.0;
    std::sort(v.begin(), v.end());
    const std::size_t m = v.size() / 2;
    if (v.size() % 2 == 1) {
        return v[m];
    }
    return 0.5 * (v[m - 1] + v[m]);
}

double compute_mean(const std::vector<double>& v) {
    if (v.empty()) return 0.0;
    const double sum = std::accumulate(v.begin(), v.end(), 0.0);
    return sum / static_cast<double>(v.size());
}

BenchmarkRow run_single_benchmark(
    const std::string& dataset_name,
    const std::string& text,
    int repetitions,
    int max_naive_n
) {
    const int n = static_cast<int>(text.size());
    BenchmarkRow row;
    row.dataset = dataset_name;
    row.n = n;

    eda::DC3Stats dc3_stats{};
    eda::SAISStats sais_stats{};
    const std::vector<int> sa_dc3 = eda::build_dc3_sa(text, &dc3_stats);
    const std::vector<int> sa_sais = eda::build_sais_sa(text, &sais_stats);
    assert(sa_dc3 == sa_sais && "Error critico en benchmark: DC3 y SA-IS difieren.");

    if (n <= max_naive_n) {
        const auto t0 = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        const std::vector<int> sa_naive = eda::build_naive_sa(text);
        const auto t1 = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        row.naive_ms = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t1 - t0).count();
        assert(sa_sais == sa_naive && "Error critico en benchmark: SA-IS y Naive difieren.");
    }

    std::vector<double> dc3_times;
    std::vector<double> sais_times;
    dc3_times.reserve(repetitions);
    sais_times.reserve(repetitions);

    for (int r = 0; r < repetitions; ++r) {
        eda::DC3Stats st_dc3{};
        auto out_dc3 = eda::build_dc3_sa(text, &st_dc3);
        (void)out_dc3;
        dc3_times.push_back(st_dc3.elapsed_ms);
        dc3_stats = st_dc3;

        eda::SAISStats st_sais{};
        auto out_sais = eda::build_sais_sa(text, &st_sais);
        (void)out_sais;
        sais_times.push_back(st_sais.elapsed_ms);
        sais_stats = st_sais;
    }

    row.dc3_median_ms = compute_median(dc3_times);
    row.dc3_mean_ms = compute_mean(dc3_times);
    row.sais_median_ms = compute_median(sais_times);
    row.sais_mean_ms = compute_mean(sais_times);
    row.speedup_dc3_over_sais = (row.sais_median_ms > 0.0) ? (row.dc3_median_ms / row.sais_median_ms) : 0.0;

    row.dc3_max_depth = dc3_stats.max_recursion_depth;
    row.sais_max_depth = sais_stats.max_recursion_depth;
    row.dc3_reduction_ratio = dc3_stats.first_level_reduction_ratio;
    row.sais_reduction_ratio = sais_stats.first_level_reduction_ratio;

    row.dc3_peak_aux_kb = dc3_stats.peak_aux_bytes / 1024;
    row.sais_peak_aux_kb = sais_stats.peak_aux_bytes / 1024;
    row.dc3_bytes_per_char = dc3_stats.bytes_per_char;
    row.sais_bytes_per_char = sais_stats.bytes_per_char;
    row.sais_compact_bpc = sais_stats.compact_bytes_per_char;

    return row;
}

void write_benchmark_csv(const std::string& csv_path, const std::vector<BenchmarkRow>& results) {
    std::ofstream csv(csv_path);
    if (!csv) return;
    csv << "dataset,n,naive_ms,dc3_median_ms,dc3_mean_ms,sais_median_ms,sais_mean_ms,"
           "speedup_dc3_over_sais,dc3_max_depth,sais_max_depth,dc3_reduction_ratio,"
           "sais_reduction_ratio,dc3_peak_aux_kb,sais_peak_aux_kb,dc3_bytes_per_char,"
           "sais_bytes_per_char,sais_compact_bpc\n";
    for (const auto& r : results) {
        csv << r.dataset << ","
            << r.n << ","
            << std::fixed << std::setprecision(4)
            << r.naive_ms << ","
            << r.dc3_median_ms << ","
            << r.dc3_mean_ms << ","
            << r.sais_median_ms << ","
            << r.sais_mean_ms << ","
            << r.speedup_dc3_over_sais << ","
            << r.dc3_max_depth << ","
            << r.sais_max_depth << ","
            << r.dc3_reduction_ratio << ","
            << r.sais_reduction_ratio << ","
            << r.dc3_peak_aux_kb << ","
            << r.sais_peak_aux_kb << ","
            << r.dc3_bytes_per_char << ","
            << r.sais_bytes_per_char << ","
            << r.sais_compact_bpc << "\n";
    }
}

int run_cache_profiler_mode(const std::string& csv_path) {
    std::cout << "========================================================================================================\n";
    std::cout << " PERFILADOR DE JERARQUIA DE CACHE L1D (32KB) / L2 (256KB): DC3 vs SA-IS\n";
    std::cout << "========================================================================================================\n\n";

    struct CaseSpec {
        std::string name;
        int kind;
    };
    const std::vector<CaseSpec> families = {
        {"random_ascii", 0},
        {"dna_genome",   1},
        {"source_code",  2},
        {"periodic_str", 3}
    };
    const std::vector<int> cache_sizes = {10000, 50000, 100000, 200000};

    std::ofstream csv(csv_path);
    csv << "dataset,n,dc3_total_accesses,dc3_indirect_pct,dc3_l1_misses,dc3_l1_miss_rate_pct,"
           "dc3_l2_misses,dc3_l1_misses_per_char,sais_total_accesses,sais_indirect_pct,"
           "sais_l1_misses,sais_l1_miss_rate_pct,sais_l2_misses,sais_l1_misses_per_char,"
           "l1_miss_ratio_dc3_over_sais,l2_miss_ratio_dc3_over_sais\n";

    std::cout << "Familia       |       n | DC3 L1 Miss/ch (Rate%) | SAIS L1 Miss/ch (Rate%) | DC3/SAIS L1 Miss | DC3/SAIS L2 Miss\n";
    std::cout << "-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n";

    for (const auto& fam : families) {
        for (int n : cache_sizes) {
            std::string text;
            if (fam.kind == 0) text = generate_random_text(n, 64);
            else if (fam.kind == 1) text = generate_dna_sequence(n);
            else if (fam.kind == 2) text = generate_source_code(n);
            else text = generate_periodic_string(n);

            // Simular DC3
            CacheHierarchySimulator sim_dc3;
            {
                std::vector<int> s(n + 3, 0);
                for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) s[i] = static_cast<int>(static_cast<unsigned char>(text[i])) + 1;
                std::vector<int> SA(n, 0);
                sim_dc3_suffixArray(s.data(), SA.data(), n, 256, sim_dc3);
            }

            // Simular SA-IS
            CacheHierarchySimulator sim_sais;
            {
                std::vector<int> s(n + 1, 0);
                for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) s[i] = static_cast<int>(static_cast<unsigned char>(text[i])) + 1;
                std::vector<int> SA(n + 1, -1);
                sim_sais_core(s.data(), SA.data(), n, 256, sim_sais);
            }

            const double dc3_ind_pct = 100.0 * static_cast<double>(sim_dc3.indirect_accesses) / sim_dc3.total_accesses;
            const double dc3_l1_rate = 100.0 * static_cast<double>(sim_dc3.l1_misses) / sim_dc3.total_accesses;
            const double dc3_l1_mpc  = static_cast<double>(sim_dc3.l1_misses) / n;

            const double sais_ind_pct = 100.0 * static_cast<double>(sim_sais.indirect_accesses) / sim_sais.total_accesses;
            const double sais_l1_rate = 100.0 * static_cast<double>(sim_sais.l1_misses) / sim_sais.total_accesses;
            const double sais_l1_mpc  = static_cast<double>(sim_sais.l1_misses) / n;

            const double l1_ratio = static_cast<double>(sim_dc3.l1_misses) / std::max<std::uint64_t>(1, sim_sais.l1_misses);
            const double l2_ratio = static_cast<double>(sim_dc3.l2_misses) / std::max<std::uint64_t>(1, sim_sais.l2_misses);

            std::cout << std::left << std::setw(13) << fam.name << std::right << " | "
                      << std::setw(7) << n << " | "
                      << std::setw(10) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << dc3_l1_mpc
                      << " (" << std::setw(5) << dc3_l1_rate << "%)   | "
                      << std::setw(11) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << sais_l1_mpc
                      << " (" << std::setw(5) << sais_l1_rate << "%)   | "
                      << std::setw(15) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << l1_ratio << "x | "
                      << std::setw(15) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << l2_ratio << "x\n";

            csv << fam.name << "," << n << ","
                << sim_dc3.total_accesses << "," << std::fixed << std::setprecision(4) << dc3_ind_pct << ","
                << sim_dc3.l1_misses << "," << dc3_l1_rate << "," << sim_dc3.l2_misses << "," << dc3_l1_mpc << ","
                << sim_sais.total_accesses << "," << sais_ind_pct << ","
                << sim_sais.l1_misses << "," << sais_l1_rate << "," << sim_sais.l2_misses << "," << sais_l1_mpc << ","
                << l1_ratio << "," << l2_ratio << "\n";
        }
    }
    std::cout << "-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n";
    std::cout << "[OK] Metricas de cache exportadas a: " << csv_path << "\n";
    return 0;
}

} // namespace

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    const int repetitions = 5;
    const int max_naive_n = 10000;

    // Modo 1: Perfilado de cache (--profile-cache <output_csv>)
    if (argc >= 3 && std::string(argv[1]) == "--profile-cache") {
        return run_cache_profiler_mode(argv[2]);
    }

    // Modo 2: Evaluacion exclusiva de archivos de corpus (--only-files <output_csv> <file1> ...)
    if (argc >= 4 && std::string(argv[1]) == "--only-files") {
        const std::string out_csv = argv[2];
        std::vector<BenchmarkRow> file_results;
        std::cout << "========================================================================================================\n";
        std::cout << " BENCHMARK SOBRE ARCHIVOS DE CORPUS EN data/\n";
        std::cout << "========================================================================================================\n";
        std::cout << "Archivo                      |        n |  DC3(ms) | SAIS(ms) | Speedup | Depth(D/S) | Ratio(D / S) | B/char(D/S/Comp)\n";
        std::cout << "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n";
        for (int argi = 3; argi < argc; ++argi) {
            const std::string filepath = argv[argi];
            std::ifstream fin(filepath, std::ios::binary);
            if (!fin) continue;
            std::ostringstream buffer;
            buffer << fin.rdbuf();
            const std::string content = buffer.str();
            if (content.empty()) continue;

            const BenchmarkRow r = run_single_benchmark(filepath, content, repetitions, max_naive_n);
            file_results.push_back(r);
            std::cout << std::left << std::setw(28) << r.dataset << std::right << " | "
                      << std::setw(8) << r.n << " | "
                      << std::setw(8) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.dc3_median_ms << " | "
                      << std::setw(8) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.sais_median_ms << " | "
                      << std::setw(6) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.speedup_dc3_over_sais << "x | "
                      << std::setw(4) << r.dc3_max_depth << " / " << std::setw(3) << r.sais_max_depth << " | "
                      << std::setw(5) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << r.dc3_reduction_ratio << " / "
                      << std::setw(5) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << r.sais_reduction_ratio << " | "
                      << std::setw(5) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.dc3_bytes_per_char << "/"
                      << std::setw(5) << r.sais_bytes_per_char << "/"
                      << std::setw(4) << r.sais_compact_bpc << "\n";
        }
        std::cout << "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n";
        write_benchmark_csv(out_csv, file_results);
        std::cout << "[OK] Resultados de corpus exportados a: " << out_csv << "\n";
        return 0;
    }

    // Modo por defecto: Suite completa de escalamiento por familias + archivos opcionales
    std::cout << "========================================================================================================\n";
    std::cout << " ETAPA 4: BENCHMARK EXPERIMENTAL DC3 (Karkkainen & Sanders) vs SA-IS (Nong, Zhang & Chan)\n";
    std::cout << "========================================================================================================\n\n";

    const std::vector<int> sizes = {2000, 10000, 50000, 100000, 500000, 1000000, 2000000};
    std::vector<BenchmarkRow> results;

    struct FamilySpec {
        std::string name;
        int kind;
    };
    const std::vector<FamilySpec> families = {
        {"random_ascii", 0},
        {"dna_genome",   1},
        {"source_code",  2},
        {"periodic_str", 3}
    };

    for (const auto& fam : families) {
        std::cout << ">>> Distribucion: " << fam.name << " (" << repetitions << " repeticiones por punto)\n";
        std::cout << "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n";
        std::cout << "       n | Naive(ms) |  DC3(ms) | SAIS(ms) | Speedup | Depth(D/S) | Ratio(D / S) |  B/char (DC3 / SAIS / Comp)\n";
        std::cout << "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n";

        for (int n : sizes) {
            std::string text;
            if (fam.kind == 0) text = generate_random_text(n, 64);
            else if (fam.kind == 1) text = generate_dna_sequence(n);
            else if (fam.kind == 2) text = generate_source_code(n);
            else text = generate_periodic_string(n);

            const BenchmarkRow r = run_single_benchmark(fam.name, text, repetitions, max_naive_n);
            results.push_back(r);

            std::cout << std::setw(8) << r.n << " | ";
            if (r.naive_ms >= 0.0) {
                std::cout << std::setw(9) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.naive_ms << " | ";
            } else {
                std::cout << "      --- | ";
            }
            std::cout << std::setw(8) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.dc3_median_ms << " | "
                      << std::setw(8) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.sais_median_ms << " | "
                      << std::setw(6) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.speedup_dc3_over_sais << "x | "
                      << std::setw(4) << r.dc3_max_depth << " / " << std::setw(3) << r.sais_max_depth << " | "
                      << std::setw(5) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << r.dc3_reduction_ratio << " / "
                      << std::setw(5) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << r.sais_reduction_ratio << " | "
                      << std::setw(6) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.dc3_bytes_per_char << " / "
                      << std::setw(6) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.sais_bytes_per_char << " / "
                      << std::setw(5) << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << r.sais_compact_bpc << "\n";
        }
        std::cout << "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n";
    }

    const std::string csv_path = "results/benchmark_metrics.csv";
    write_benchmark_csv(csv_path, results);
    std::cout << "[OK] Resultados exportados exitosamente a: " << csv_path << "\n";

    const std::size_t os_rss_kb = get_os_peak_rss_bytes() / 1024;
    if (os_rss_kb > 0) {
        std::cout << "[INFO] Pico de memoria RSS del proceso reportado por el SO: " << os_rss_kb << " KB ("
                  << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << (static_cast<double>(os_rss_kb) / 1024.0) << " MB)\n";
    }

    return 0;
}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/prepare_corpus.py
Genera los 4 archivos de corpus experimentales en data/ (texto aleatorio, genoma ADN,
codigo fuente C++ real del proyecto y cadenas altamente periodicas) y ejecuta el
binario compilado build/benchmark.exe para exportar results/corpus_benchmark.csv.
"""

from pathlib import Path
import random
import subprocess
import sys


def build_random_corpus(target_len: int, seed: int = 42) -> str:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    alphabet = [chr(33 + i) for i in range(64)]
    return "".join(rng.choice(alphabet) for _ in range(target_len))


def build_dna_corpus(target_len: int, seed: int = 1337) -> str:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    bases = ["A", "C", "G", "T"]
    motifs = [
        "GATTACAGATTACATGCA",
        "TATATATATATATATATA",
        "CGCGCGCGCGCGCGCGCG",
        "ATGAAAGCCCTTTGGGAAATTTCCC",
    ]
    chunks = []
    current_len = 0
    while current_len < target_len:
        if rng.random() < 0.18:
            m = rng.choice(motifs)
            chunks.append(m)
            current_len += len(m)
        else:
            b = rng.choice(bases)
            chunks.append(b)
            current_len += 1
    return "".join(chunks)[:target_len]


def build_source_code_corpus(repo_root: Path, target_len: int, seed: int = 2026) -> str:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    source_files = [
        repo_root / "include" / "naive_sa.hpp",
        repo_root / "include" / "dc3.hpp",
        repo_root / "src" / "dc3.cpp",
        repo_root / "include" / "sais.hpp",
        repo_root / "src" / "sais.cpp",
        repo_root / "tests" / "test_runner.cpp",
        repo_root / "bench" / "benchmark.cpp",
        repo_root / "scripts" / "prepare_corpus.py",
        repo_root / "scripts" / "profile_cache.py",
        repo_root / "scripts" / "plot_results.py",
    ]
    chunks = []
    current_len = 0
    for p in source_files:
        if p.exists():
            text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            chunks.append(text)
            current_len += len(text)

    # Generar modulos C++ variados (estados de parser LR(1), AST y rutinas de tabla de simbolos)
    # sin repetir de forma identica el mismo bloque completo.
    types = ["int", "std::size_t", "uint32_t", "int64_t", "double", "bool"]
    ops = ["+", "-", "^", "&", "|", "*"]
    tokens = ["TOK_IDENT", "TOK_NUM", "TOK_LPAREN", "TOK_RPAREN", "TOK_ASSIGN", "TOK_SEMI", "TOK_EOF"]
    mod_id = 0
    while current_len < target_len:
        t_ret = rng.choice(types)
        t_arg = rng.choice(types)
        op1, op2 = rng.choice(ops), rng.choice(ops)
        tok_a, tok_b = rng.choice(tokens), rng.choice(tokens)
        k1 = rng.randint(3, 251)
        k2 = rng.randint(100, 9999)
        snippet = (
            f"\n// Modulo Sintetico de Analisis Lexico/Sintactico #{mod_id}\n"
            f"namespace lexer_unit_{mod_id} {{\n"
            f"static constexpr int STATE_BASE_{mod_id} = {k2};\n"
            f"inline {t_ret} transition_step_{mod_id}({t_arg} sym, int state, const int* bkt) {{\n"
            f"    int next_s = (state {op1} {k1}) {op2} static_cast<int>(sym);\n"
            f"    if (sym == {mod_id % 127} && next_s > 0) {{\n"
            f"        return static_cast<{t_ret}>(bkt[next_s & 0xFF] + STATE_BASE_{mod_id});\n"
            f"    }} else if (sym == {(mod_id * 7) % 127}) {{\n"
            f"        for (int i = 0; i < {(mod_id % 16) + 4}; ++i) {{\n"
            f"            next_s = (next_s * 31 + i) ^ {k1};\n"
            f"        }}\n"
            f"        return static_cast<{t_ret}>(next_s); // {tok_a} -> {tok_b}\n"
            f"    }}\n"
            f"    return static_cast<{t_ret}>(0);\n"
            f"}}\n"
            f"}} // namespace lexer_unit_{mod_id}\n"
        )
        chunks.append(snippet)
        current_len += len(snippet)
        mod_id += 1

    return "".join(chunks)[:target_len]


def build_periodic_corpus(target_len: int) -> str:
    # Palabra de Fibonacci combinada con patrones clasicos de los papers
    f0, f1 = "b", "a"
    while len(f1) < 4096:
        f0, f1 = f1, f1 + f0
    pattern = f1 + "_mississippi_mmiissiissiippii_abracadabra_"
    repeats = (target_len // len(pattern)) + 2
    return (pattern * repeats)[:target_len]


def main() -> int:
    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    data_dir = repo_root / "data"
    results_dir = repo_root / "results"
    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    target_size = 1_000_000
    corpus_specs = [
        ("corpus_random.txt", build_random_corpus(target_size)),
        ("corpus_dna.fasta", build_dna_corpus(target_size)),
        ("corpus_source_code.txt", build_source_code_corpus(repo_root, target_size)),
        ("corpus_periodic.txt", build_periodic_corpus(target_size)),
    ]

    saved_paths = []
    print("==================================================================")
    print(" PASO 8.1: Generacion de Corpus Experimentales en data/")
    print("==================================================================")
    for filename, content in corpus_specs:
        out_path = data_dir / filename
        out_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
        unique_chars = len(set(content))
        print(f" [OK] {out_path.relative_to(repo_root)} -> {len(content)} bytes (|Sigma| = {unique_chars})")
        saved_paths.append(str(out_path.relative_to(repo_root)).replace("\\", "/"))

    bench_exe = repo_root / "build" / ("benchmark.exe" if sys.platform.startswith("win") else "benchmark")
    if not bench_exe.exists():
        print(f"[ERROR] No se encontro el ejecutable {bench_exe}. Compilalo primero.")
        return 1

    out_csv = "results/corpus_benchmark.csv"
    cmd = [str(bench_exe), "--only-files", out_csv] + saved_paths
    print("\nEjecutando benchmark sobre los archivos de data/...")
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(repo_root), check=False)
    return proc.returncode


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/profile_cache.py
Ejecuta el simulador de jerarquia de cache L1D (32 KB, 8-way, lineas de 64B) y
L2 (256 KB, 8-way, lineas de 64B) integrado en build/benchmark.exe y analiza
la localidad espacial/temporal de DC3 frente a SA-IS.
"""

import csv
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys


def main() -> int:
    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    results_dir = repo_root / "results"
    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    bench_exe = repo_root / "build" / ("benchmark.exe" if sys.platform.startswith("win") else "benchmark")
    if not bench_exe.exists():
        print(f"[ERROR] No se encontro el ejecutable {bench_exe}. Compilalo primero.")
        return 1

    out_csv = "results/cache_metrics.csv"
    cmd = [str(bench_exe), "--profile-cache", out_csv]
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(repo_root), check=False)
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        return proc.returncode

    csv_file = repo_root / out_csv
    if not csv_file.exists():
        return 1

    with csv_file.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))

    print("\nResumen de Localidad Espacial (n = 200,000):")
    print("----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
    print("Distribucion  | Accesos Indirectos (DC3 vs SAIS) | Fallos L1 / char (DC3 vs SAIS) | Reduccion Fallos")
    print("----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
    for r in rows:
        if int(r["n"]) == 200000:
            ds = r["dataset"]
            dc3_ind = float(r["dc3_indirect_pct"])
            sais_ind = float(r["sais_indirect_pct"])
            dc3_mpc = float(r["dc3_l1_misses_per_char"])
            sais_mpc = float(r["sais_l1_misses_per_char"])
            ratio = float(r["l1_miss_ratio_dc3_over_sais"])
            print(
                f"{ds:<13} | {dc3_ind:>12.2f}% vs {sais_ind:>11.2f}%   | "
                f"{dc3_mpc:>11.2f} vs {sais_mpc:>11.2f}    | {ratio:>12.2f}x menos"
            )
    print("----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/plot_results.py
Lee los archivos CSV de results/ (benchmark_metrics.csv, corpus_benchmark.csv y
cache_metrics.csv) y genera las 4 figuras comparativas en alta resolucion (.png):
  1. results/fig1_time_scaling.png
  2. results/fig2_speedup_by_family.png
  3. results/fig3_memory_and_recursion.png
  4. results/fig4_cache_and_corpus.png

Incluye soporte automatico para pandas/matplotlib y un motor grafico cientifico PNG
nativo en Python estandar (zlib + struct) para garantizar ejecucion 100% reproducible
en entornos Windows/MSYS2 sin dependencias externas.
"""

import csv
import math
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import zlib


# =============================================================================
# Motor de Renderizado Cientifico PNG en Python Puro (sin dependencias externas)
# =============================================================================

class PureCanvasPNG:
    def __init__(self, width: int, height: int, bg=(255, 255, 255)):
        self.width = width
        self.height = height
        row_bytes = bytes(bg) * width
        self.buf = bytearray(row_bytes * height)

    def set_pixel(self, x: int, y: int, color):
        if 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height:
            idx = (y * self.width + x) * 3
            self.buf[idx] = color[0]
            self.buf[idx + 1] = color[1]
            self.buf[idx + 2] = color[2]

    def fill_rect(self, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int, color):
        xa, xb = max(0, min(x0, x1)), min(self.width - 1, max(x0, x1))
        ya, yb = max(0, min(y0, y1)), min(self.height - 1, max(y0, y1))
        if xa > xb or ya > yb:
            return
        span = xb - xa + 1
        row_chunk = bytes(color) * span
        for y in range(ya, yb + 1):
            start = (y * self.width + xa) * 3
            self.buf[start : start + span * 3] = row_chunk

    def draw_rect(self, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int, color, thickness: int = 1):
        self.fill_rect(x0, y0, x1, y0 + thickness - 1, color)
        self.fill_rect(x0, y1 - thickness + 1, x1, y1, color)
        self.fill_rect(x0, y0, x0 + thickness - 1, y1, color)
        self.fill_rect(x1 - thickness + 1, y0, x1, y1, color)

    def draw_line(self, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int, color, thickness: int = 2):
        dx = abs(x1 - x0)
        dy = -abs(y1 - y0)
        sx = 1 if x0 < x1 else -1
        sy = 1 if y0 < y1 else -1
        err = dx + dy
        r = thickness // 2
        while True:
            if r <= 0:
                self.set_pixel(x0, y0, color)
            else:
                self.fill_rect(x0 - r, y0 - r, x0 + r, y0 + r, color)
            if x0 == x1 and y0 == y1:
                break
            e2 = 2 * err
            if e2 >= dy:
                err += dy
                x0 += sx
            if e2 <= dx:
                err += dx
                y0 += sy

    def draw_circle(self, cx: int, cy: int, radius: int, color):
        for dy in range(-radius, radius + 1):
            for dx in range(-radius, radius + 1):
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius:
                    self.set_pixel(cx + dx, cy + dy, color)

    def draw_text(self, x: int, y: int, text: str, color=(20, 20, 20), scale: int = 2):
        cx = x
        for ch in text.upper():
            glyph = FONT_5X7.get(ch, FONT_5X7[" "])
            for row_idx, row_bits in enumerate(glyph):
                for col_idx in range(5):
                    if (row_bits >> (4 - col_idx)) & 1:
                        px = cx + col_idx * scale
                        py = y + row_idx * scale
                        self.fill_rect(px, py, px + scale - 1, py + scale - 1, color)
            cx += 6 * scale

    def save_png(self, filepath: Path):
        raw_rows = bytearray()
        stride = self.width * 3
        for y in range(self.height):
            raw_rows.append(0)  # Filter type 0 (None)
            start = y * stride
            raw_rows.extend(self.buf[start : start + stride])

        def make_chunk(chunk_type: bytes, data: bytes) -> bytes:
            crc = zlib.crc32(chunk_type + data) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            return struct.pack(">I", len(data)) + chunk_type + data + struct.pack(">I", crc)

        ihdr = struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", self.width, self.height, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0)
        idat = zlib.compress(bytes(raw_rows), level=6)
        png_bytes = (
            b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
            + make_chunk(b"IHDR", ihdr)
            + make_chunk(b"IDAT", idat)
            + make_chunk(b"IEND", b"")
        )
        if filepath.exists():
            filepath.unlink()
        filepath.write_bytes(png_bytes)


# =============================================================================
# Funciones de Graficacion para las 4 Figuras del Informe
# =============================================================================

COLOR_DC3 = (214, 48, 49)      # Rojo intenso para DC3
COLOR_SAIS = (9, 132, 227)     # Azul brillante para SA-IS
COLOR_COMP = (0, 184, 148)     # Verde esmeralda para SA-IS Compacto
COLOR_NAIVE = (108, 92, 231)   # Violeta para Naive SA
COLOR_GRID = (230, 234, 240)
COLOR_AXIS = (60, 65, 75)


def plot_fig1_time_scaling(rows, out_path: Path):
    canvas = PureCanvasPNG(1400, 920, bg=(252, 253, 255))
    canvas.draw_text(140, 25, "FIG 1: ESCALAMIENTO DE TIEMPO DE CONSTRUCCION (DC3 VS SA-IS)", (20, 30, 45), scale=3)
    canvas.draw_text(280, 60, "TIEMPO MEDIANO (MS) EN FUNCION DE N (4 FAMILIAS DE ENTRADA)", (80, 90, 105), scale=2)

    families = ["random_ascii", "dna_genome", "source_code", "periodic_str"]
    boxes = [
        (90, 110, 650, 460),
        (760, 110, 1320, 460),
        (90, 530, 650, 880),
        (760, 530, 1320, 880),
    ]

    for fam, (x0, y0, x1, y1) in zip(families, boxes):
        sub = [r for r in rows if r["dataset"] == fam]
        if not sub:
            continue
        canvas.fill_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, (255, 255, 255))
        canvas.draw_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_AXIS, thickness=2)
        canvas.draw_text(x0 + 15, y0 - 22, f"FAMILIA: {fam.upper()}", (30, 40, 55), scale=2)

        max_n = max(int(r["n"]) for r in sub)
        max_y = max(max(float(r["dc3_median_ms"]), float(r["sais_median_ms"])) for r in sub) * 1.15
        if max_y <= 0:
            max_y = 1.0

        # Lineas de grilla horizontales
        for g in range(1, 5):
            gy = y1 - int((y1 - y0) * g / 5.0)
            canvas.draw_line(x0 + 2, gy, x1 - 2, gy, COLOR_GRID, thickness=1)
            val = max_y * g / 5.0
            canvas.draw_text(x0 - 65, gy - 6, f"{val:5.1f}", (90, 90, 90), scale=2)

        def to_xy(n_val, t_val):
            px = x0 + 20 + int((x1 - x0 - 40) * (n_val / max_n))
            py = y1 - 15 - int((y1 - y0 - 35) * (t_val / max_y))
            return px, py

        dc3_pts = [to_xy(int(r["n"]), float(r["dc3_median_ms"])) for r in sub]
        sais_pts = [to_xy(int(r["n"]), float(r["sais_median_ms"])) for r in sub]

        for i in range(len(dc3_pts) - 1):
            canvas.draw_line(dc3_pts[i][0], dc3_pts[i][1], dc3_pts[i + 1][0], dc3_pts[i + 1][1], COLOR_DC3, thickness=3)
            canvas.draw_line(sais_pts[i][0], sais_pts[i][1], sais_pts[i + 1][0], sais_pts[i + 1][1], COLOR_SAIS, thickness=3)

        for (px, py), r in zip(dc3_pts, sub):
            canvas.draw_circle(px, py, 5, COLOR_DC3)
        for (px, py), r in zip(sais_pts, sub):
            canvas.draw_circle(px, py, 5, COLOR_SAIS)

        # Etiquetas del ultimo punto (n = 2M)
        canvas.draw_text(dc3_pts[-1][0] - 85, dc3_pts[-1][1] - 18, f"DC3:{float(sub[-1]['dc3_median_ms']):.1f}MS", COLOR_DC3, scale=2)
        canvas.draw_text(sais_pts[-1][0] - 95, sais_pts[-1][1] + 10, f"SAIS:{float(sub[-1]['sais_median_ms']):.1f}MS", COLOR_SAIS, scale=2)
        canvas.draw_text(x0 + 10, y1 + 8, "N=2K..500K", (90, 90, 90), scale=2)
        canvas.draw_text(x1 - 80, y1 + 8, "N=2M", (90, 90, 90), scale=2)

    canvas.save_png(out_path)


def plot_fig2_speedup_by_family(rows, out_path: Path):
    canvas = PureCanvasPNG(1300, 760, bg=(252, 253, 255))
    canvas.draw_text(130, 28, "FIG 2: SPEEDUP DE SA-IS SOBRE DC3 (TIEMPO DC3 / TIEMPO SA-IS)", (20, 30, 45), scale=3)
    canvas.draw_text(260, 62, "VALORES > 1.0X INDICAN QUE SA-IS ES MAS RAPIDO QUE DC3", (80, 90, 105), scale=2)

    x0, y0, x1, y1 = 120, 110, 1220, 660
    canvas.fill_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, (255, 255, 255))
    canvas.draw_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_AXIS, thickness=2)

    families = [
        ("random_ascii", (108, 92, 231)),
        ("dna_genome", (0, 184, 148)),
        ("source_code", (9, 132, 227)),
        ("periodic_str", (214, 48, 49)),
    ]
    sizes = [2000, 10000, 50000, 100000, 500000, 1000000, 2000000]
    max_sp = 2.5

    for g in range(1, 6):
        val = 0.5 * g
        gy = y1 - int((y1 - y0) * (val / max_sp))
        col = (150, 150, 150) if abs(val - 1.0) < 1e-6 else COLOR_GRID
        canvas.draw_line(x0 + 2, gy, x1 - 2, gy, col, thickness=2 if abs(val - 1.0) < 1e-6 else 1)
        canvas.draw_text(x0 - 70, gy - 6, f"{val:4.1f}X", (60, 60, 60), scale=2)

    for idx_s, s_val in enumerate(sizes):
        gx = x0 + 60 + int((x1 - x0 - 120) * idx_s / (len(sizes) - 1))
        canvas.draw_line(gx, y0 + 2, gx, y1 - 2, COLOR_GRID, thickness=1)
        label = f"{s_val // 1000000}M" if s_val >= 1000000 else f"{s_val // 1000}K"
        canvas.draw_text(gx - 20, y1 + 12, label, (60, 60, 60), scale=2)

    for fam_idx, (fam, col) in enumerate(families):
        sub = [r for r in rows if r["dataset"] == fam]
        pts = []
        for idx_s, r in enumerate(sub):
            gx = x0 + 60 + int((x1 - x0 - 120) * idx_s / (len(sizes) - 1))
            sp = float(r["speedup_dc3_over_sais"])
            gy = y1 - int((y1 - y0) * (sp / max_sp))
            pts.append((gx, gy, sp))
        for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
            canvas.draw_line(pts[i][0], pts[i][1], pts[i + 1][0], pts[i + 1][1], col, thickness=3)
        for gx, gy, sp in pts:
            canvas.draw_circle(gx, gy, 5, col)

        # Leyenda superior dentro del grafico
        lx = x0 + 30 + fam_idx * 265
        ly = y0 + 25
        canvas.fill_rect(lx, ly, lx + 24, ly + 12, col)
        canvas.draw_text(lx + 32, ly, f"{fam.upper()} ({pts[-1][2]:.2f}X)", col, scale=2)

    canvas.save_png(out_path)


def plot_fig3_memory_and_recursion(rows, out_path: Path):
    canvas = PureCanvasPNG(1400, 820, bg=(252, 253, 255))
    canvas.draw_text(110, 25, "FIG 3: CONSUMO DE MEMORIA (B/CHAR), RECURSION Y RAZON DE REDUCCION", (20, 30, 45), scale=3)
    max_n = max(int(r["n"]) for r in rows)
    canvas.draw_text(270, 58, f"COMPARATIVA EN N = {max_n:,} SOBRE LAS 4 DISTRIBUCIONES", (80, 90, 105), scale=2)

    sub500 = [r for r in rows if int(r["n"]) == max_n]

    # Panel Izquierdo: Bytes por caracter (DC3 vs SA-IS 32b vs SA-IS Compacto)
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = 80, 115, 690, 720
    canvas.fill_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, (255, 255, 255))
    canvas.draw_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_AXIS, thickness=2)
    canvas.draw_text(x0 + 20, y0 - 22, "A) PICO DE MEMORIA RAM (BYTES / CARACTER)", (30, 40, 55), scale=2)

    max_bpc = 28.0
    for g in range(1, 5):
        val = 7.0 * g
        gy = y1 - int((y1 - y0 - 40) * (val / max_bpc))
        canvas.draw_line(x0 + 2, gy, x1 - 2, gy, COLOR_GRID, thickness=1)
        canvas.draw_text(x0 - 50, gy - 6, f"{int(val)}B", (80, 80, 80), scale=2)

    for idx, r in enumerate(sub500):
        base_x = x0 + 35 + idx * 140
        b_dc3 = float(r["dc3_bytes_per_char"])
        b_sais = float(r["sais_bytes_per_char"])
        b_comp = float(r["sais_compact_bpc"])

        for b_idx, (val, col) in enumerate([(b_dc3, COLOR_DC3), (b_sais, COLOR_SAIS), (b_comp, COLOR_COMP)]):
            bx0 = base_x + b_idx * 34
            bh = int((y1 - y0 - 40) * (val / max_bpc))
            canvas.fill_rect(bx0, y1 - bh, bx0 + 28, y1 - 2, col)
            canvas.draw_text(bx0 - 2, y1 - bh - 16, f"{val:.1f}", col, scale=1)

        short_name = r["dataset"].split("_")[0].upper()
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 10, y1 + 12, short_name, (50, 50, 50), scale=2)

    canvas.draw_text(x0 + 20, y0 + 18, "ROJO: DC3 (~23B) | AZUL: SAIS INT (~14B) | VERDE: SAIS COMPACTO (~5.3B)", (40, 40, 40), scale=1)

    # Panel Derecho: Profundidad Recursiva y Razon de Reduccion (n02/n vs n1/n)
    rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1 = 760, 115, 1350, 720
    canvas.fill_rect(rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1, (255, 255, 255))
    canvas.draw_rect(rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1, COLOR_AXIS, thickness=2)
    canvas.draw_text(rx0 + 20, ry0 - 22, "B) PROFUNDIDAD RECURSIVA Y REDUCCION (%)", (30, 40, 55), scale=2)

    max_depth = max([max(int(r["dc3_max_depth"]), int(r["sais_max_depth"])) for r in sub500] + [8]) * 1.25
    for idx, r in enumerate(sub500):
        base_x = rx0 + 35 + idx * 140
        d_dc3 = int(r["dc3_max_depth"])
        d_sais = int(r["sais_max_depth"])
        rat_dc3 = float(r["dc3_reduction_ratio"]) * 100.0
        rat_sais = float(r["sais_reduction_ratio"]) * 100.0

        h_dc3 = int((ry1 - ry0 - 80) * (d_dc3 / max_depth))
        h_sais = int((ry1 - ry0 - 80) * (d_sais / max_depth))

        canvas.fill_rect(base_x, ry1 - h_dc3, base_x + 42, ry1 - 2, COLOR_DC3)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 6, ry1 - h_dc3 - 20, f"D={d_dc3}", COLOR_DC3, scale=2)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 2, ry1 - h_dc3 - 36, f"{rat_dc3:.0f}%", COLOR_DC3, scale=1)

        canvas.fill_rect(base_x + 50, ry1 - h_sais, base_x + 92, ry1 - 2, COLOR_SAIS)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 56, ry1 - h_sais - 20, f"D={d_sais}", COLOR_SAIS, scale=2)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 52, ry1 - h_sais - 36, f"{rat_sais:.0f}%", COLOR_SAIS, scale=1)

        short_name = r["dataset"].split("_")[0].upper()
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 10, ry1 + 12, short_name, (50, 50, 50), scale=2)

    canvas.draw_text(rx0 + 20, ry0 + 18, "ETIQUETAS: D = NIVELES RECURSIVOS | % = RAZON DE REDUCCION (67% VS ~33%)", (40, 40, 40), scale=1)
    canvas.save_png(out_path)


def plot_fig4_cache_and_corpus(cache_rows, corpus_rows, out_path: Path):
    canvas = PureCanvasPNG(1400, 820, bg=(252, 253, 255))
    canvas.draw_text(120, 25, "FIG 4: FALLOS DE CACHE L1 (CACHE MISSES) Y DESEMPENO EN DATA/", (20, 30, 45), scale=3)
    canvas.draw_text(250, 58, "SIMULACION L1D (32KB, LINEA 64B) Y TIEMPO REAL EN ARCHIVOS DE CORPUS", (80, 90, 105), scale=2)

    # Panel Izquierdo: Fallos de cache L1 por caracter en n = 200,000
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = 80, 115, 690, 720
    canvas.fill_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, (255, 255, 255))
    canvas.draw_rect(x0, y0, x1, y1, COLOR_AXIS, thickness=2)
    canvas.draw_text(x0 + 20, y0 - 22, "A) FALLOS DE CACHE L1 POR CARACTER (N=200K)", (30, 40, 55), scale=2)

    c200 = [r for r in cache_rows if int(r["n"]) == 200000]
    max_mpc = max([float(r["dc3_l1_misses_per_char"]) for r in c200] + [1.0]) * 1.25

    for idx, r in enumerate(c200):
        base_x = x0 + 35 + idx * 140
        m_dc3 = float(r["dc3_l1_misses_per_char"])
        m_sais = float(r["sais_l1_misses_per_char"])

        h_dc3 = int((y1 - y0 - 60) * (m_dc3 / max_mpc))
        h_sais = int((y1 - y0 - 60) * (m_sais / max_mpc))

        canvas.fill_rect(base_x, y1 - h_dc3, base_x + 42, y1 - 2, COLOR_DC3)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 2, y1 - h_dc3 - 20, f"{m_dc3:.2f}", COLOR_DC3, scale=2)

        canvas.fill_rect(base_x + 50, y1 - h_sais, base_x + 92, y1 - 2, COLOR_SAIS)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 52, y1 - h_sais - 20, f"{m_sais:.2f}", COLOR_SAIS, scale=2)

        short_name = r["dataset"].split("_")[0].upper()
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 10, y1 + 12, short_name, (50, 50, 50), scale=2)

    # Panel Derecho: Tiempo real (ms) en los 4 archivos de data/
    rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1 = 760, 115, 1350, 720
    canvas.fill_rect(rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1, (255, 255, 255))
    canvas.draw_rect(rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1, COLOR_AXIS, thickness=2)
    canvas.draw_text(rx0 + 20, ry0 - 22, "B) TIEMPO EN ARCHIVOS DE CORPUS DATA/ (MS)", (30, 40, 55), scale=2)

    max_ms = max([float(r["dc3_median_ms"]) for r in corpus_rows] + [1.0]) * 1.25
    for idx, r in enumerate(corpus_rows):
        base_x = rx0 + 35 + idx * 140
        t_dc3 = float(r["dc3_median_ms"])
        t_sais = float(r["sais_median_ms"])

        h_dc3 = int((ry1 - ry0 - 60) * (t_dc3 / max_ms))
        h_sais = int((ry1 - ry0 - 60) * (t_sais / max_ms))

        canvas.fill_rect(base_x, ry1 - h_dc3, base_x + 42, ry1 - 2, COLOR_DC3)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 2, ry1 - h_dc3 - 20, f"{t_dc3:.1f}", COLOR_DC3, scale=2)

        canvas.fill_rect(base_x + 50, ry1 - h_sais, base_x + 92, ry1 - 2, COLOR_SAIS)
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 52, ry1 - h_sais - 20, f"{t_sais:.1f}", COLOR_SAIS, scale=2)

        fname = Path(r["dataset"]).stem.replace("corpus_", "").split("_")[0].upper()
        canvas.draw_text(base_x + 6, ry1 + 12, fname, (50, 50, 50), scale=2)

    canvas.save_png(out_path)


def main() -> int:
    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    results_dir = repo_root / "results"

    bench_csv = results_dir / "benchmark_metrics.csv"
    corpus_csv = results_dir / "corpus_benchmark.csv"
    cache_csv = results_dir / "cache_metrics.csv"

    if not bench_csv.exists() or not corpus_csv.exists() or not cache_csv.exists():
        print("[ERROR] Faltan archivos CSV en results/. Ejecuta prepare_corpus.py y profile_cache.py primero.")
        return 1

    with bench_csv.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        bench_rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    with corpus_csv.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        corpus_rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    with cache_csv.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        cache_rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))

    f1 = results_dir / "fig1_time_scaling.png"
    f2 = results_dir / "fig2_speedup_by_family.png"
    f3 = results_dir / "fig3_memory_and_recursion.png"
    f4 = results_dir / "fig4_cache_and_corpus.png"

    plot_fig1_time_scaling(bench_rows, f1)
    plot_fig2_speedup_by_family(bench_rows, f2)
    plot_fig3_memory_and_recursion(bench_rows, f3)
    plot_fig4_cache_and_corpus(cache_rows, corpus_rows, f4)

    print("==================================================================")
    print(" PASO 8.3: Exportacion de Graficas Cientificas en results/")
    print("==================================================================")
    for p in (f1, f2, f3, f4):
        print(f" [OK] {p.relative_to(repo_root)} ({p.stat().st_size // 1024} KB)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

// Modulo Sintetico de Analisis Lexico/Sintactico #0
namespace lexer_unit_0 {
static constexpr int STATE_BASE_0 = 1781;
inline int transition_step_0(uint32_t sym, int state, const int* bkt) {
    int next_s = (state | 246) | static_cast<int>(sym);
    if (sym == 0 && next_s > 0) {
        return static_cast<int>(bkt[next_s & 0xFF] + STATE_BASE_0);
    } else if (sym == 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            next_s = (next_s * 31 + i) ^ 246;
        }
        return static_cast<int>(next_s); // TOK_SEMI -> TOK_EOF
    }
    return static_cast<int>(0);
}
} // namespace lexer_unit_0

// Modulo Sintetico de Analisis Lexico/Sintactico #1
namespace lexer_unit_1 {
static constexpr int STATE_BASE_1 = 9075;
inline std::size_t transition_step_1(double sym, int state, const int* bkt) {
    int next_s = (state | 149) | static_cast<int>(sym);
    if (sym == 1 && next_s > 0) {
        return static_cast<std::size_t>(bkt[next_s & 0xFF] + STATE_BASE_1);
    } else if (sym == 7) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
            next_s = (next_s * 31 + i) ^ 149;
        }
        return static_cast<std::size_t>(next_s); // TOK_RPAREN -> TOK_EOF
    }
    return static_cast<std::size_t>(0);
}
} // namespace lexer_unit_1

    if (sym == 2 && next_s > 0) {
        return static_cast<bool>(bkt[next_s & 0xFF] + STATE_BASE_2);
    } else if (sym == 14) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i) {
            next_s = (next_s * 31 + i) ^ 160;
        }
        return static_cast<bool>(next_s); // TOK_NUM -> TOK_IDENT
    }
    return static_cast<bool>(0);
}
} // namespace lexer_unit_2

        return static_cast<double>(next_s); // TOK_ASSIGN -> TOK_LPAREN

    return static_cast<double>(0);

// Modulo Sintetico de Analisis Lexico/Sintactico #1407
namespace lexer_unit_1407 {
static constexpr int STATE_BASE_1407 = 4331;
inline double transition_step_1407(double sym, int state, const int* bkt) {
    int next_s = (state - 211) * static_cast<int>(sym);
    if (sym == 10 && next_s > 0) {
        return static_cast<double>(bkt[next_s & 0xFF] + STATE_BASE_1407);
    } else if (sym == 70) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 19; ++i) {
            next_s = (next_s * 31 + i) ^ 211;
        }
        return static_cast<double>(next_s); // TOK_NUM -> TOK_NUM
    }
    return static_cast<double>(0);
}
} // namespace lexer_unit_1407

    int next_s = (state
